# Current event catalogue: A-F

Every row is a generated Event declaration in at least one pinned target. Source presence does not certify frame registration or addon access. `CallbackEvent` declarations use a different dispatch mechanism. Follow the source or retrieve the full typed payload and restrictions with the [query tool](../../wow-api-index/scripts/query.mjs); the [event skill](../SKILL.md) explains usage.
